════════ FABLE-1 THEATER-GATE — CORRECTED ANALYSIS (EMIT-anchored parse) ════════
Engine-native run: aiden pool, hexa v0.546.0, hexa run cli/anima_ablate.hexa -- d768.clm, EXIT_RC=0
Real path: cli/anima.hexa rel_ctx build (L2300-2312, 43-way mean incl agloop_ctx/CR3 wire)
  -> rel -> brain_decide_anchored (core/brain.hexa, exact fn brain_emit calls) -> should_emit.
4 arms, IDENTICAL per-tick inputs (cur,coh_lane,bal_lane,idle,anchors,pf), differ ONLY in rel_ctx.
N=200 ticks, fixed seed 'zephyrine...', ultradian stage schedule dr_stage_at(tick*8).

emit_live=10/200 (10 WAKE/drive_hi ticks emit; 190 SLEEP ticks silent)
Hamming frozen(0.5) vs live = 0  (0.00% of N)
Hamming zero(0.0)   vs live = 0  (0.00% of N)
Hamming shuffle     vs live = 0  (0.00% of N)
ticks where ANY arm disagrees = 0
PRE-REGISTERED bar: theater CONFIRMED iff Hamming(frozen,live)<5%N AND Hamming(zero,live)<5%N (<10).
RESULT: 0 and 0 -> THEATER CONFIRMED = TRUE (read-side rel_ctx INERT on emit/silence).

psi = pure_field_phi(pf) = 0.118983 CONSTANT all ticks/arms (pf warmed once, READ-only).
  mean|psi-0.5| = 0.381017 ; psi deviation BETWEEN arms = 0 (pf shared, never stepped).

MECHANISM (reference-matched, core/engine_g.hexa + core/brain.hexa):
  motivation = 0.20*rel + 0.10*0.6 + 0.15*cur + 0 + 0.10*coh + 0.10*0.5 + 0.15*bal + 0.10*1.0 + anchor_nudge
  emit = (motivation > 0.3) AND safe;  safe = kill & rate(idle>=30) & phi_ratchet & content
  WAKE: live motiv ~0.74, ZERO-rel motiv ~0.58 -> BOTH >> 0.3 -> emit=1 (rel cannot pull below thr)
  SLEEP: drive_hi=false -> idle=5 < 30 -> rate_limit FALSE -> safe=false -> emit=0 (rel irrelevant)
  => decision 100% governed by 'safe' conjunction (stage->drive_hi->rate-limit) + motivation FLOOR
     that already clears 0.3 without rel. The 0.20*rel term never straddles the threshold.
  Shuffle==live by construction (equal-weight mean is permutation-invariant): rel_ctx encodes only
     aggregate MAGNITUDE, zero STRUCTURE -- and that magnitude is itself INERT on the decision.
